The JDK restructure moved tools into the JDK modules system."," ","-The profile activation mechanism checks if the file exists:","-  <activation>","-    <file>","-      <exists>${java.home}/../lib/tools.jar</exists>","-    </file>","-  </activation>","+SOLUTION FOR ERROR 2:","+- Add a dependencyManagement section to override the tools.jar dependency","+- Point to ${java.home}/lib/modules instead of the non-existent tools.jar","+- Add JDK version constraints to Maven profiles to prevent Java <9 from using incorrect paths","+- Create a java9-plus-profile that doesn't reference tools.jar"," ","-When running on Java 11 (openjdk-11), the file /usr/lib/jvm/java-11-openjdk-amd64/../lib/tools.jar does NOT exist. This causes the profile not to activate, but somewhere a dependency or plugin is still trying to resolve com.sun:tools:jar:11.0.27 (which was likely declared for the toolsjar property).","+ROOT CAUSE ERROR 1:","+-------------------","+The @Generated annotation is missing from the javax.annotation package. This annotation was part of the Java standard library but has been moved to a separate module/library in Java 9+."," ","+In Java 8, @Generated came from javax.annotation package (from tools.jar).","+In Java 9+, the annotation is not automatically available and must be added as an explicit dependency.","+"," AFFECTED FILES:"," ---------------","-1. /home/travis/build/failed/google/auto/pom.xml (lines 167-207)","-   - Contains two profiles that define toolsjar property","-   - The default-profile looks for tools.jar in ${java.home}/../lib/tools.jar","-   - The mac-profile looks for classes.jar in ${java.home}/../Classes/classes.jar","+1. /home/travis/build/failed/google/auto/pom.xml","+   - Needs dependencyManagement section for com.sun:tools","+   - Needs JDK version constraints on profiles"," ","-SOLUTION:","----------","-For Java 9+, tools.jar no longer exists and is not needed. All modules (common, factory, service, value)","+   - Need explicit dependency on javax.annotation for Java 9+ support"," ","-The fix involves modifying the parent pom.xml to:","-1.
